Reports; does not fix. ([Finefactory-ai/foreman](https://github.com/Finefactory-ai/foreman/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md))
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Reviews Apex, triggers, and LWC against GForce standards — fflib layering, DataAccess.USER_MODE, bulkification, governor limits, NebulaLogger, 90% coverage. TRIGGER when: reviewing a diff or file containing .cls, .trigger, or LWC changes. DO NOT TRIGGER when: the change is TypeScript, Terraform, workflow YAML, or Dockerfiles, even in a repo that also contains Apex. ([Gforce-Innovation-Kft/sf-develop-demo](https://github.com/Gforce-Innovation-Kft/sf-develop-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Use for independent review of the actual diff against contracts, principles and evidence, with cited anchors. Reviews; never authors the work under review. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-codebase-scanner**: Analyzes codebase for concerns, quality issues, and improvement opportunities. Writes SCAN.md directly. ([ivkan/specflow-cc](https://github.com/ivkan/specflow-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/codebase-scanner.md) · ★1)
- **sf-context-librarian**: Select the smallest SfSkills skill/reference set for an observed Salesforce deployment failure. Returns paths, reasons, and estimated context cost. Does not diagnose. - **sf-context-loader**: SFプロジェクトの docs/ からタスク内容に関連するコンテキストのみを選択的に抽出して要約するエージェント。通常モード（docs/ 全体を構造化マッチングでスキャン）と knowledge-only モード（backlog.md〔investigator の分〕・backlog-planner から focus_hints:["knowledge-only"] で呼ばれ case-index/pitfalls/sf-standard/decisions の4ファイル限定 Grep）の2モードをサポート。backlog系・release-preparer・sf-architect・assistant 等から Phase 0 として呼ばれる。無関係なタスクや docs/ 未整備プロジェクトには「該当コンテキストなし」を返す。knowledge-only モードが参照するのは docs/knowledge/ のキュレーション済みナレッジ文書であり、Backlog の実課題データ・docs/logs/ 対応実績ログという一次情報の参照は pattern-curator が担当する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-context-loader.md))
- **sf-coverage**: You are the **Test Coverage** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. ([iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude](https://github.com/iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-coverage.md) · ★4)
- **sf-critic**: Review agent. Multi-depth code review — quick for small changes, deep for complex. Traces imports and data flow. ([shipfast-ai/shipfast](https://github.com/shipfast-ai/shipfast/blob/HEAD/agents/critic.md) · ★8)
- **sf-critic**: 완성된 원고 전체(또는 큰 덩어리)를 문예 비평가의 시선으로 평가한다. 구조, 인물 아크, 주제의 정합성, SF 아이디어가 인간의 이야기로 작동하는지를 본다. ([Ssojux2/novel-wiki](https://github.com/Ssojux2/novel-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-critic.md))

- **sf-deploy-engineer**: Runs wave-3 Salesforce deployments - validate first, then quick-deploy the recorded job id. - **sf-design-reviewer**: Use when a plan or diff needs an anti-bloat design review. Applies the four sf- design skills: reuse first, the smallest complete diff, no unrequested abstraction. - **sf-design-step3**: sf-design コマンドから委譲される機能一覧ステップ。generate_feature_list.py を直接呼び出して機能一覧.xlsx を生成する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-design-step3.md))
- **sf-design-writer**: プログラム設計書（Excel）と機能一覧（Excel）を生成する専門エージェント。sf-design-step2 エージェントから委譲されて実行する。force-app/ と docs/ を徹底的に読み込み、高品質な設計内容 JSON を生成してから Python スクリプトで Excel に変換する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-design-writer.md))
- **sf-detail-design-writer**: 詳細設計書（Excel）を業務グループ単位で生成する専門エージェント。feature_groups.yml が示すグループ構成とソースコードを読み込み、エンジニア向けの詳細設計 JSON を生成してから Python スクリプトで Excel に変換する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-detail-design-writer.md))
- **sf-developer**: Developer of the Software Factory cell. Default implementer for general (non-ML) application code. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-developer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-developer**: Build stage of the Foreman pipeline for Salesforce projects. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-devops-engineer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **sf-doc-objects-writer**: sf-doc コマンドから委譲されてオブジェクト項目定義書を生成する。Salesforce組織に直接接続して xlsx を 01_基本設計/ に出力し、両方選択時は sf-doc-overview-writer を連鎖呼び出しする。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-doc-objects-writer.md))
- **sf-doc-overview-writer**: sf-doc コマンドから委譲されるプロジェクト概要書生成エージェント。docs/ 配下の情報源を元に generate_basic_doc.py を呼び出してプロジェクト概要書.xlsx を生成する。単独実行（概要書のみ選択）または両方選択時の sf-doc-objects-writer からの連鎖呼び出しで起動する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-doc-overview-writer.md))
- **sf-docs**: Answers a question from official Salesforce docs (developer, architect, help, admin and lightningdesignsystem.com) with verbatim quotes the script copies from the cached page, each tied to its official URL and line. Use it proactively whenever the user wants something read, scanned, checked or looked up in Salesforce documentation, even without naming this agent ("what does Help say about X", "check the Apex guide for Y", "scan the docs on Z"), and when you do not know which page holds the answer. Brief it with the question, the site or product if known, and the width ("the one concept page"… ([drsaavedra/sf-docs-agent](https://github.com/drsaavedra/sf-docs-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-docs.md) · ★4)
- **sf-driver**: Drives SentinelFlow WC3 instances for the repetitive, context-heavy work — booting instances, screenshotting, recapturing/verifying anchors, walking phase flows, and live-testing the behaviour driver. Use this whenever a task means many screenshots, crop/verify iterations, or multi-step instance navigation, so that churn stays out of the main context. Returns a terse summary (outcome + diagnostics), never frame/log dumps. Spawn with model sonnet (default); pass haiku for trivial mechanical runs. ([coller95/sentinelflow](https://github.com/coller95/sentinelflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-driver.md))
- **sf-edit**: SaaSFoundry edit tier: claude-haiku-5-5 at high effort. Use for scoped-edit (a small change confined to one or two files). Do not use for exploration, prose (use sf-light); bug-fix, review (use sf-fix); multi-file-feature, tests (use sf-build); planning (use sf-plan); terminal-ci (use sf-terminal). ([DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI](https://github.com/DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-edit.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sf-effort-estimator**: 工数見積専用エージェント。`/backlog` フェーズ内外を問わず「工数計算 / 工数見積 / 工数 / effort / 見積 / 何時間」系の問い全てを担当する。作業の型（作業の大きさで分けた10種）が同じ過去の課題を case-index.md と Backlog から選び、Backlog の実績時間で確かめた値の中央値から `{N}h` + 根拠 + 信頼度を返す（似た課題が足りなければ global-calibration.md の対応する種別の中央値）。頼まれたときだけ、合計を変えずにお客様向けの内訳に配分する。直接呼び出し可。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-effort-estimator.md))

- **sf-org-verifier**: Verifies a Salesforce change in the live org after deployment - smoke probes, data and limit queries, log capture, and diagnostics collection. Use in wave 4 to confirm the story actually works in the org and to recommend forward-fix or rollback. ([grzmol/vibe-force](https://github.com/grzmol/vibe-force/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-org-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **sf-orphaned**: You are the **Orphaned Metadata** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. Your job is to detect inactive, deactivated, and stale metadata that is cluttering the org without delivering value — a common source of confusion, slow deployments, and technical debt. ([iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude](https://github.com/iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-orphaned.md) · ★4)
- **sf-plan**: SaaSFoundry plan tier: claude-opus-5-5 at high effort. Use for planning (planning a ticket, a design or an approach). Do not use for exploration, prose (use sf-light); scoped-edit (use sf-edit); bug-fix, review (use sf-fix); multi-file-feature, tests (use sf-build); terminal-ci (use sf-terminal). ([DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI](https://github.com/DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-plan.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **sf-principal-engineer**: Use for high-risk or complex implementation: auth, money, state machines, migrations, concurrency. - **sf-product-manager**: Product Manager (PdM) of the Software Factory cell. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-product-manager.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-product-manager**: Use when intent needs to become outcomes, scope and acceptance criteria. Owns product outcomes and story acceptance criteria; uses the selected BMad PM/PRD planning route. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-product-manager.md))
- **sf-project-inspector**: Optionally discover or validate a local Salesforce DX project for triage. Read-only. Never treats the SfSkills checkout as a DX project. - **sf-project-inspector**: Optionally discover an external Salesforce DX project and map deployment or test evidence to local metadata paths. - **sf-project-manager**: Project Manager (PM) of the Software Factory cell. - **sf-qa**: Independent QA for a Salesforce change before it ships. Pulls the Jira ticket and the approved spec, derives falsifiable test cases, runs them against the build or QA org, checks for logged errors, and reports pass/fail with explicit caveats. Use from /sf-ticket (build mode, then qa mode), or on its own: "QA SF-123", "test this ticket in staging", "verify this fix in the sandbox". ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-qa-engineer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-qa-engineer**: Use for independent verification of acceptance criteria on the actual HEAD: effects, recovery, regression and compatibility. - **sf-refactor-specialist**: Refactor/Rewrite Specialist of the Software Factory cell. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-refactor-specialist.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-release-engineer**: Use for CI/CD, pipelines, gated sequential merges and deploy-SHA proof. Acts as the single integration owner when the orchestrator delegates a merge. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-release-engineer.md))
- **sf-repo-investigator**: Read-only investigator for the Salesforce source repo and change history during /sf-ticket — references to in-scope API names, the automation's source logic, recent commits and Gearset deployments touching it, open worktrees, and repo-vs-org drift. - **sf-runtime-specialist**: Analisar Glue, EMR, runtimes, capacidade, infraestrutura e compatibilidade entre versoes numa migracao. ([EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws](https://github.com/EdgarSocrates98/spark-forge-aws/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-runtime-specialist.md) · 5 copies)
